- **spec-guide**: Create role-based instructions for a feature delivered by a spec, split into developer, admin, and user guidance, then verify every numbered step through its real delivery vehicle. - **spec-guided-tdd**: Guides spec-guided, test-driven development where an LLM agent implements code against a test suite it does not read. Covers writing a function spec sheet and a hidden test spec sheet, generating a paired holdout/implementer (_IMP) test suite, implementing in a fresh session, and verifying with holdout tests. Use this skill whenever the user wants to write a spec sheet, function spec, test spec, holdout tests, or wants LLM-written code they can rely on without the agent cheating on tests, even if they only say "spec first", "TDD with an agent", or "let's start a new function the spec way".
